&\quad +\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\Big\}\label{R:eq:in-GII.I2}
\end{align}
with $\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\KK(x-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\Big[\KK(z-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:Aanti} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x$, $x'$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{c}(x')]=i f^{d'ch}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{d'bh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-1-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{d}(x')]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hm}(x')\,U^{ld}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-2-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{c}(x')]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{ae}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{al}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-3-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{d}(x')]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GII.I2-4-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GII.I2}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hm}(x')\,U^{ld}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{ae}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{al}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}\label{R:eq:res-GII.I2}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)&=\KK(x-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\Big[\KK(z-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\\bar K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)&=\KK(x-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\Big[\KK(z-w')\cdot\KK(x-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\Big]
\end{align*}

\section{Group III (with c.c.)}\label{R:sec:G3}
\subsection{Group III, Row I}\label{R:sec:GIII.I}
\emph{Input.} Prefactor $-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{ig^4}{8\pi^5}\frac1{k^+}\log\frac\vee\Lambda=-2i\NN$, kernel $K_{\mathfrak B}$. Order of the charges: $\rho(x')\rho(y)\rho(x)$.
\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.I}={}&-2i\,\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{R:eq:in-GIII.I}
\end{align}
with $K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)]=i f^{b'd'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'd'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{lm}(y)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{md}(y)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)]=i f^{b'd'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{b'd'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{c'e'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x')\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{c'e'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{le}(x')\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{c'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{c'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{c'e'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x')\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{c'e'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{c'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{c'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.I-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.I}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{lm}(y)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{md}(y)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{le}(x')\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:res-GIII.I}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')&=\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\\K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)&=\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)&=\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row II}\label{R:sec:GIII.II}
\emph{Input.} Prefactor $-2i\NN$, kernel $K_{\mathfrak B}$. Order of the charges: $\rho(y)\rho(x')\rho(x)$.
\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.II}={}&-2i\,\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{R:eq:in-GIII.II}
\end{align}
with $K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $y$, $x'$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{d'b'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'b'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{ea}(w)\,U^{hl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{l}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{d'b'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{d'b'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{bb'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{bb'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{bde}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{bdh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{bdh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{bb'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{bb'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(y)\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{beh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{beh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.II-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.II}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{ea}(w)\,U^{hl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{l}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{bde}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(y)\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(y)\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:res-GIII.II}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)&=\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(y-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)&=\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]\\K_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)&=\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row III, first part}\label{R:sec:GIII.III}
\emph{Input.} Prefactor $-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{ig^4f^{c'd'a}}{16\pi^5}\frac1{k^+}\log\frac\vee\Lambda=-i\NN f^{c'd'a}$. After the $w'$ integration the measured gluon of the conjugate amplitude sits at $y'$: the phase is $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}$, and the kernel is $K_{\mathfrak C}\equiv\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)$.
\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.III}={}&-i\,\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{R:eq:in-GIII.III}
\end{align}
with $K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:Aanti} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-1-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-2-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-3-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.III-4-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.III}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\Big\}+\big(y'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:res-GIII.III}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\\K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row V}\label{R:sec:GIII.V}
\emph{Input.} Prefactor $-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^3}\frac{ig^4f^{c'ca}}{8\pi^5}\frac1{k^+}\log\frac\vee\Lambda=-2i\NN f^{c'ca}$, phase $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}$, kernel $K_{\mathfrak C}$. Order of the charges: $\rho(x')\rho(x)\rho(y)$.
\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.V}={}&-2i\,\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{R:eq:in-GIII.V}
\end{align}
with $K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $x$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(y')\,U^{bc}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(w)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(y')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.V-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.V}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(y')\,U^{bc}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(w)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(y')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(y'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:res-GIII.V}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\\K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\\K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x-w)
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row VI}\label{R:sec:GIII.VI}
\emph{Input.} Prefactor $-2i\NN f^{c'd'a}$, phase $e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}$, kernel $K_{\mathfrak C}$. Order of the charges: $\rho(x')\rho(y)\rho(x)$.
\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.VI}={}&-2i\,\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{R:eq:in-GIII.VI}
\end{align}
with $K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{deh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{deh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(y')\,U^{bc}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{dbh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(y')\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{dbh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{deh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{deh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{dbh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{dbh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{R:eq:GIII.VI-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NN$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.VI}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(y'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(y')\,U^{bc}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(y'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:res-GIII.VI}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\\K_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\\K_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)&=\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)
\end{align*}

\section{Summary: all two-$\rho$ terms}\label{R:sec:summary}
Below are all two-$\rho$ terms generated by the reordering, with the kernels written out. Every coefficient is real. For Groups I and III the complex conjugate is included as the exchange of the two measured-gluon positions in the phase, shown at the end of each line. The four-$\rho$ remainders and Group II have no c.c.\ (Sec.~\ref{sec:conv}). The symmetric three-$\rho$ remainders are not listed (Sec.~\ref{R:sec:identity}).
\subsection*{Three-$\rho$ terms from reordering the four-$\rho$ terms}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm A1}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dae}\,U^{dh}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{dh}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{bc}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dae}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hd}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hd}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\Big\}\label{R:eq:sum-A1}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm A2}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^2}\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^2}\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{bc}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{a}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{bh}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{bh}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\Big\}\label{R:eq:sum-A2}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm B1}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{db}(w')\,U^{lc}(y)\,U^{ae}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{db}(w')\,U^{ec}(x')\,U^{al}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{bc}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\

\subsection*{Group I (with c.c.)}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GI.II}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x',y)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x,x')\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x',y)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x,x')\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x',y)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x,x')\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x',y)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK^{i}(x'-w')\,\mathfrak F^{i}(w;x,x')\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:sum-GI.II}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GI.III}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\Phi(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\Phi(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\Phi(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\Phi(x')\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\Phi(x)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:sum-GI.III}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GI.IV}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]\notag\\

&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ld}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{me}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\,\Big[\KK(z-w)\cdot\KK(y-z)+\tfrac12\,\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\Big]\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:sum-GI.IV}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3N^{(2\rho)}}{d^3k}\bigg|_{\rm GI.V1}={}&\NN\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-z)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(z-x)\cdot\KK(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(z-x)\cdot\KK(x'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{be}(x)\,U^{cl}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(z-x)\cdot\KK(y'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y')\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(z-x)\cdot\KK(x'-x)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(z-x)\cdot\KK(y'-x)\notag\\

&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y')\,U^{bc}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(y')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\KK(y'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\,\KK(x'-y')\cdot\KK(y-w)\notag\\
&\qquad\times f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(y'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{R:eq:sum-GIII.VI}
\end{align}



\section{Region $p^+\gg k^+$: reordering the three-$\rho$ terms}\label{app:reorderP}
The three-$\rho$ terms proportional to $\log(\vee/k^+)$ are $B=P-A$ for the rows of Sec.~\ref{B:sec:rows} with three charges. The reordering is linear, so the two-$\rho$ terms of $B$ are those of $P$ minus those of $A$:
\begin{itemize}
\item The two-$\rho$ terms of $A$ (the $\log(\vee/\Lambda)$ three-$\rho$ terms) are worked out step by step in Apps.~\ref{R:sec:fourrho}--\ref{R:sec:G3} (with the corrections of Part~\ref{part:three}: $\mathcal D$, and the $\alpha$ part of Row II). They enter here with a minus sign.
\item The two-$\rho$ terms of $P$ are worked out below in the same way, for every $P$ source. There the coefficient of $\log\vee$ is written as $P|_{\rm source}$, in units of $\NP$, like $B$. The colour structures are those of the corresponding $A$ source, only the kernel changes (for Group III Rows III, V, VI also $U(y')\to U(z)$).
\end{itemize}
As written, Group I Rows II and V have $P=0$, and their $B=-A$; with F4, Row II has $P=-\alpha$ (Sec.~\ref{sec:alpha}). The fix P1 is included in the source ``Group I, Row III (region P)'' below: its kernel is $K^{P}_{\rm I.III}=-K_b+2K_h$, i.e.\ the corrected one. The kernels at the coincident points are listed after each source.

\subsection{Group I, Row III ($\bar{\mathbb B}_3^\dagger\mathbb A$) (region P)}\label{B:sec:GI.III.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GI.III.P}={}&i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GI.III.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[-\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $x$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{b'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{b'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{ech}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{ech}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x')\,U^{bc}(y)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{b'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(x')\,f^{b'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{ech}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(x)\,f^{ech}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{b'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{b'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{ech}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,f^{ech}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{c}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x')\,U^{bc}(y)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{b'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(x')\,f^{b'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{ech}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abd}\,U^{ab'}(w')\,U^{de}(x)\,U^{bc}(x)\,f^{ech}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.III.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GI.III.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac14\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac14\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GI.III.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x',y)&=\,\Big[-\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x')&=\,\Big[-\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\rm I.III}(x',x,x)&=\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\,\Big[-\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)+2\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group I, Row IV ($\bar{\mathbb A}^\dagger\mathbb B_2$) (region P)}\label{B:sec:GI.IV.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GI.IV.P}={}&2i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{c'}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{c'}(x)\notag\\
