&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GI.IV.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{c'}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c'}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c'}(x)]=i f^{b'c'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,f^{b'c'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c'}(x)]=i f^{ec'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,f^{ec'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cd}(x)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x')\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(y)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{c'}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c'}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ld}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{me}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{c'}(x)]=i f^{b'c'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,f^{b'c'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c'}(x)]=i f^{ec'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{cdc'}\,U^{ac}(w)\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{be}(y)\,f^{ec'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cd}(x)\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(x')\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(w')\,f^{abc}\,U^{be}(y)\,U^{cd}(y)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GI.IV.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $2i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GI.IV.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ld}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{me}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GI.IV.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')&=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)&=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)&=\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group II, Row I, first term (region P)}\label{B:sec:GII.I1.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GII.I1.P}={}&-i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big\}\label{B:eq:in-GII.I1.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:antiA} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x$, $x'$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{c'}(x')]=i f^{dc'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{dc'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{dbh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{dbh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-1-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{c'}(x')]=i f^{ec'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{ec'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(w')\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{ebh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{ebh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-2-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{d'}(x')]=i f^{dd'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x)\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{dd'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ld}(x)\,U^{hm}(y)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(x),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{de'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(x)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{de'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-3-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d'}(x')]=i f^{ed'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x)\,U^{c'e'}(y)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{ed'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{ee'h}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad -\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{ad'}(x')\,U^{c'e'}(x)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{ee'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{-\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I1.P-4-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GII.I1.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(w')\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ld}(x)\,U^{hm}(y)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}\label{B:eq:res-GII.I1.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)&=\,\Big[\KK(x-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)&=\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group II, Row I, second term (region P)}\label{B:sec:GII.I2.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GII.I2.P}={}&i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\Big\}\label{B:eq:in-GII.I2.P}
\end{align}
with $\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(z-w')\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x-w')\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:Aanti} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x$, $x'$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{c}(x')]=i f^{d'ch}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{d'bh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-1-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{d'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{d}(x')]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(x),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'bd'}\,U^{ac'}(w')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hm}(x')\,U^{ld}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-2-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{c}(x')]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{ae}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{b}(y)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{al}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-3-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{d}(x'),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{d}(x')]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-x'),\quad x'\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x),\rho^{e}(y)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{ac'd'}\,U^{bc'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GII.I2.P-4-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GII.I2.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{lm}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{hm}(x')\,U^{ld}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{ae}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{cd}(x)\,U^{al}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}\label{B:eq:res-GII.I2.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x,x,y)&=\,\Big[\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(z-w')\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x-w')\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\\bar K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x)&=\frac{1}{(x-z)^2}\,\Big[\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(z-w')-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x-w')\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row I (region P)}\label{B:sec:GIII.I.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GIII.I.P}={}&-2i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GIII.I.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)]=i f^{b'd'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'd'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{lm}(y)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{md}(y)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)]=i f^{b'd'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(x')\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{b'd'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{c'b}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{c'e'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x')\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{c'e'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{le}(x')\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{c'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{c'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{c'}(x')\,\rho^{e'}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)]=i f^{c'e'h}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(x')\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{c'e'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{c'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{c'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{c'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{d'e'}(y)\,U^{ac'}(w')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{c'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.I.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.I.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{da}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{l}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{lm}(y)\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{m}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{md}(y)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(z)\,U^{le}(x')\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(w')\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bl}(y)\,U^{ad}(w')\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(w')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GIII.I.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,x')&=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)&=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)&=\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row II (region P)}\label{B:sec:GIII.II.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GIII.II.P}={}&-2i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GIII.II.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x,y)=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $y$, $x'$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{d'b'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'b'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{ea}(w)\,U^{hl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{l}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{d'dh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{d'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{d'}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{d'b'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{d'b'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{d'}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{d'eh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{d'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{c'd'}(y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,U^{bc'}(z)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d'}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{bb'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{bb'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{bde}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{bdh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{bdh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)]=i f^{b'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{cbd}\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{b'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,\rho^{b}(y)\,\rho^{b'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')]=i f^{bb'h}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x'),\quad x'\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab'}(y)\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x)\,f^{bb'h}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(y)\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{b}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{beh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(y)\,f^{beh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{b'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{b'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{b'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab'}(x')\,f^{acd}\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{de}(x')\,f^{b'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.II.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.II.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{bd}(z)\,U^{ea}(w)\,U^{hl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{l}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{la}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{m}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hl}(y)\,U^{md}(x')\,U^{bh}(z)\,U^{ma}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{bde}\,U^{hd}(y)\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(y),\rho^{c}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{e}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ae}(y)\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{cl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{db}(z)\,U^{ce}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(y)\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GIII.II.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(y,x,y)&=\,\Big[\KK(y-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(y-w')\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',y,y)&=\frac{1}{(y-z)^2}\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(y-w)\Big]\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak B}(x',x',y)&=\,\Big[\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(z-w)\,\KK(y-z)\cdot\KK(x'-z)-\frac{1}{2}\,\KK(x'-w')\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)\Big]
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row III, first part (region P)}\label{B:sec:GIII.III.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GIII.III.P}={}&-i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GIII.III.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:Aanti} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ca}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2$^\star$)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-1-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-2-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)]=i f^{e'ch}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'ch}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ac}(w)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{c}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-3-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac12\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac12\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac12\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.III.P-4-AC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.III.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ca}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{lb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(w)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GIII.III.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^2}
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row V (region P)}\label{B:sec:GIII.V.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GIII.V.P}={}&-2i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GIII.V.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $x$, $y$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(w)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{ad}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\,\rho^{d}(y)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{e}(x),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{edh}\rho^{h}(x)\,\delta^{(2)}(x-y),\quad y\to x\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{c'ca}\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(w)\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{edh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.V.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.V.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{be}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{ce}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(w)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{la}(z)\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(w)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(w)\,U^{bd}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GIII.V.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^2}\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x)&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(x-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)
\end{align*}
\subsection{Group III, Row VI (region P)}\label{B:sec:GIII.VI.P}

\paragraph{The three-$\rho$ term.}
\begin{align}
P\bigg|_{\rm GIII.VI.P}={}&-2i\,\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\notag\\
&\quad -\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)\notag\\
&\quad +\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)\Big\}+{\rm c.c.}\label{B:eq:in-GIII.VI.P}
\end{align}
with $K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,y)=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)$.
\paragraph{Reordering.} In every sub-term (one product of the two brackets) the charges appear in the order shown, so we apply Eq.~\eqref{R:eq:plain} with $A$, $B$, $C$ the charges at $x'$, $y$, $x$. The sub-terms are numbered 1--4 in the order above. For each sub-term we list the commutators, and each resulting two-$\rho$ piece before and after the colour algebra.
\paragraph{Sub-term 1:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-1-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-1-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{deh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{deh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1$^\star$)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-1-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 2:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-2-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ca}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U2$^\star$)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-2-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{dbh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{e'c'}(z)\,U^{d'b}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{dbh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(y)\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U2$^\star$)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-2-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 3:} $-\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{e}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-3-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{e'eh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(x')\,f^{e'eh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-3-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{e}(x)]=i f^{deh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=-\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{bc}(z)\,U^{ad}(y)\,U^{ce}(y)\,f^{deh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\quad\text{(U1)},\ \text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-3-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Sub-term 4:} $+\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,\rho^{e'}(x')\,\rho^{d}(y)\,\rho^{b}(x)$.
\begin{align}
&[A,B]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)]=i f^{e'dh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-y),\quad y\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(x')\,f^{e'dh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,B],C\}}=-\tfrac14\,i N_{c}\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\quad\text{(U2)},\ \text{(F)}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-4-AB}\\
&[A,C]:\ [\rho^{e'}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{e'bh}\rho^{h}(x')\,\delta^{(2)}(x'-x),\quad x\to x'\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{e'bh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{d}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[A,C],B\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-4-AC}\\
&[B,C]:\ [\rho^{d}(y),\rho^{b}(x)]=i f^{dbh}\rho^{h}(y)\,\delta^{(2)}(y-x),\quad x\to y\notag\\
&\qquad \tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{c'd'a}\,U^{bd'}(z)\,U^{c'e'}(x')\,U^{ad}(y)\,f^{dbh}\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{e'}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\qquad\phantom{\tfrac14\{[B,C],A\}}=\tfrac14\,i\,\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}
\label{B:eq:GIII.VI.P-4-BC}
\end{align}
\paragraph{Two-$\rho$ terms of this source.} Multiplying by the prefactor $-2i\,\NP$ and collecting equal terms (dummy colour indices renamed),
\begin{align}
P^{(2\rho)}\bigg|_{\rm GIII.VI.P}={}&\NP\int e^{-ik\cdot(w'-w)}\,\Big\{\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{ce}(x')\,U^{bl}(x)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{da}(z)\,U^{cl}(y)\,U^{be}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{a}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,f^{abc}\,f^{ade}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{d}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ca}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{dbe}\,U^{ha}(y)\,U^{eh}(z)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{d}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(x)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,U^{ab}(y)\,U^{ac}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{b}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,U^{ab}(x')\,U^{ac}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{c}(y),\rho^{b}(x')\big\}\notag\\
&\quad -\tfrac12\,N_{c}\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')\,U^{ab}(z)\,U^{bc}(x')\,\big\{\rho^{c}(x'),\rho^{a}(x)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{ad}(x')\,U^{cl}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(x'),\rho^{l}(y)\big\}\notag\\
&\quad +\tfrac12\,K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{eb}(z)\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{cd}(y)\,\big\{\rho^{h}(y),\rho^{l}(x')\big\}\Big\}+\big(w'\leftrightarrow w\big)\label{B:eq:res-GIII.VI.P}
\end{align}
The kernel at the coincident points:
\begin{align*}
K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x,x')&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(x'-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(x-z)\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',x',y)&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\frac{1}{(x'-z)^2}\\K^{P}_{\mathfrak C}(x',y,y)&=\KK(w'-z)\cdot\KK(y-w)\,\KK(x'-z)\cdot\KK(y-z)
\end{align*}

%==========================================================================================

\section{Relations between colour networks}\label{app:rel}
For the kernel structures in which the simplified colour structures are linearly dependent, each dependent one equals the following combination of basis structures (exact; checked for SU(3) and SU(4)).
\subsection{Part~\ref{part:jimwlk}: coefficient of $\log(\vee/\Lambda)$, two-$\rho$}
For the kernel structures in which the simplified colour structures are linearly dependent, each dependent one equals the following combination of basis structures (exact; checked for SU(3) and SU(4)):
\paragraph{Structure 1, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(x,x,z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{dl}(w)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{hb}(x)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)+1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dl}(z)\,U^{mh}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{bd}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)+1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{bd}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)+1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(x)\,U^{md}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align*}
\paragraph{Structure 4, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(x',x',z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w)\,U^{cd}(x')\,U^{be}(z)\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)+1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x)\,U^{cd}(x')\,U^{be}(z)\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)+1\,f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')
\end{align*}
\paragraph{Structure 11, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(w,w,z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{deb}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{le}(w)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{ch}(w)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{dea}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{he}(w)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{lb}(w)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{deb}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{le}(w)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{ch}(w)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{dea}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{he}(w)\,U^{lm}(x')\,U^{lb}(w)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{c}(x)
\end{align*}
\paragraph{Structure 12, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(w',w',z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{deb}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{le}(w')\,U^{lm}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w)\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{ch}(w')\,\rho^{d}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{dea}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{he}(w')\,U^{lb}(w')\,U^{lm}(w)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{deb}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{ha}(w')\,U^{le}(w')\,U^{lm}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x)\\&\qquad=-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x)\,U^{be}(z)\,U^{ch}(w')\,\rho^{d}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)-1\,f^{abc}\,f^{dea}\,U^{hd}(z)\,U^{he}(w')\,U^{lb}(w')\,U^{lm}(x)\,\rho^{c}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x)
\end{align*}


\subsection{Part~\ref{part:bfkl}: coefficient of $\log\vee$}
\paragraph{Structure 1, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(x,x,z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{dl}(w)\,U^{lm}(w')\,U^{hb}(x)\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(w)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dl}(z)\,U^{mh}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(w')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{bd}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(w')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x')\,U^{ce}(x)\,U^{bd}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{h}(x)+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{ah}(z)\,U^{bl}(x)\,U^{dh}(x)\,U^{ml}(x')\,\rho^{m}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(x)\,U^{md}(z)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)\\&\qquad=-f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,U^{mb}(z)\,U^{md}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)-f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w)\,U^{hl}(x')\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x)\,\rho^{l}(x')\,\rho^{e}(x)
\end{align*}
\paragraph{Structure 3, $\KK(x'-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(x,x',z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=-f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{dh}(w')\,U^{hb}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')-f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{bh}(w')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(w')\,U^{ha}(x')\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{hd}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w')\,U^{ha}(x')\,\rho^{d}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&f^{abc}\,f^{dce}\,U^{ha}(x')\,U^{hd}(w)\,\rho^{b}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(w)\,U^{hd}(x')\,\rho^{a}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{ha}(w)\,\rho^{d}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')
\end{align*}
\paragraph{Structure 4, $\KK(x-w)\cdot\KK(x'-w')\;K(x',x',z)$.}
\begin{align*}
&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(w)\,U^{cd}(x')\,U^{be}(z)\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(w)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hd}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{mb}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')\\&\qquad=f^{abc}\,f^{deh}\,U^{al}(x)\,U^{cd}(x')\,U^{be}(z)\,\rho^{h}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)+f^{abc}\,f^{cde}\,U^{hb}(x')\,U^{hl}(x)\,U^{ma}(z)\,U^{md}(x')\,\rho^{l}(x)\,\rho^{e}(x')
\end{align*}

\section{Checks of the reordering}\label{R:app:checks}
The algebra was generated by a short program, which also checked it numerically.
\begin{enumerate}
\item \emph{Reordering identity.} Eqs.~\eqref{R:eq:plain}--\eqref{R:eq:antiA}, including the explicit one-$\rho$ remainder $R_1$, hold to machine precision for random matrices $A,B,C$.
\item \emph{Colour algebra.} Each of the 144 pieces was evaluated before and after the colour algebra, for $SU(3)$ with random classical charges and random symmetric adjoint Wilson lines ($U$ of $V=W\,{\rm diag}(1,-1,-1)\,W^\dagger$, $W$ random). The two agree to $2\times10^{-15}$ for every piece. With general, non-symmetric Wilson lines, 14 pieces differ. These are exactly the pieces whose simplification uses (U1$^\star$) or (U2$^\star$), i.e.\ the convention $U^{bc}=U^{cb}$.
\item \emph{The whole reordering.} For every sub-term we took the charges as explicit operators on three sites (the $\delta$-function becomes a Kronecker delta), with random kernels and random symmetric Wilson lines. We then compared
\begin{equation*}
\sum_{\rm singlets}\langle s|\,O-S(O)\,|s\rangle\qquad\text{and}\qquad\sum_{\rm singlets}\langle s|\,(\text{two-}\rho\text{ pieces})\,|s\rangle ,
\end{equation*}
where $O$ is the three-$\rho$ operator of the sub-term, $S(O)$ its fully symmetrized form, and $|s\rangle$ runs over the colour singlets, in which every one-$\rho$ term has zero expectation value. We used $SU(3)$ with one quark per site (one singlet) and $SU(2)$ with two spin-$\frac12$ per site (five singlets), three random draws each. The largest difference was $4\times10^{-14}$, for values up to $25$. This checks the commutators, the signs, the factors $\frac14$ and $\frac12$, the $\delta$-functions and the colour algebra together.
\end{enumerate}


\section{Scripts}\label{app:scripts}
The numbers and tables come from the scripts of the folders \texttt{three\_rho}, \texttt{two\_rho}, \texttt{bfkl} (see their README files) and of this folder:
\begin{itemize}
\item \texttt{ft/ft\_checks.py}: Part~\ref{part:ft} (partial fractions, the split of the bracket, the $\xi$ integral of \texttt{main.tex}, the commutator identity with explicit charges);
\item \texttt{allsets.py}: all terms by row, for the coefficients of $\log(\vee/\Lambda)$ ($A$) and of $\log\vee$ ($P$), as written and corrected;
\item \texttt{check\_all.py}: pointwise totals of $A$ vs JIMWLK$\otimes$LO and of $P$ vs BFKL$\otimes$LO (two-$\rho$; symmetrized three-$\rho$);
\item \texttt{build\_P.py} and \texttt{reduce\_run.py}: the exact tables of Secs.~\ref{B:sec:three}, \ref{B:sec:two};
\item \texttt{make\_combined.py}: writes this file from \texttt{combined\_static.tex}, \texttt{ft\_part.tex} and the notes of the other folders.
\end{itemize}
